# D01 fresh scoped invocation inputs — unexecuted source design

Project `891e7443-8dac-4c1b-897f-9e53cb59c7ee`; original D01 `a96a1977-3210-4284-8f7d-645793369301`; existing WT `42bb51c6-c198-4adb-bd92-0a5222853231`, shell `f1575610-c6f0-4dbf-9f33-d01ed057a194`. Reservation `wave30_D01_fresh_scoped_invocation_inputs_design`. Only this NEW report is owned. Entry branch `roadmap/local-module-boundaries-wave27` was clean at `292500380c4cf9ffd09042a1beba352858e578c7`; no alignment, previous-report edit, status change or runtime slot is involved. Root alone reviews, integrates and releases any memory/checkpoint execution. Future desktop remains RiWork Cua.ai Driver MCP only and HELD.

## Finding and bounded proposal

The complete frozen composition has exactly **28 load/store call sites (14 loads, 14 stores), using exactly the seven requested canonical keys**. There is no extra key or unresolved dynamic key. The two observation aliases are immutable string declarations, `pObsKey` and `OBSKEY`. The smallest proposal is an invocation-only namespace router: validate the approved public input, test all seven fresh physical keys for strict `undefined`, seed only the public release, and pass the same two scoped function references to the existing single AsyncFunction body. Every composition byte, control flow, guard and private source literal remains unchanged. The adapter neither clears nor reads the old canonical globals and never imports their seed/handles/password.

The concrete blocker is the **recorded pre-START input refusal**, not an authentication or product failure. Full public refusal appendix at immutable `be6d4afe932bf03454d6b0dedfa482c8a2e33b4f`, [user/browser report](local-wave30-d01-user-browser-review.md), lines 20387–20491: all eleven release predicates were true, but the old global `d01_preparation_seed` was non-null. No controller/archive/setup/helper/browser was invoked; its public outcome remains `root_runtime_input_unconfirmed`, `journey_credit:false`, `resource_release_proven:false`. Existing values, PIDs, password, source and origin were not inspected, inferred or cleared. This audit reads that PUBLIC Git appendix, not any current session-store value or private receipt. Unused lane release is distinct from a numeric child exit or resource-release proof.

The source guard at composition lines 171–175 remains exactly the original `!lReleaseValid(lRelease) || load("d01_preparation_seed") !== undefined && load("d01_preparation_seed") !== null`. Under the router it tests only the new scoped seed, which remains absent until the original launcher writes it at line 233. No accepted set is relaxed: the added fresh-start check refuses even a scoped `null`. An occupied namespace is abandoned, never cleared/reused by this design; root must supply a new separately approved nonce, not retry automatically.

## Complete canonical key inventory

The AST only has identifier `load` calls with one argument and identifier `store` calls with two arguments. No non-call use escapes those passed references. Aliases resolve to literal `const` declarations, with no assignment/update; the whole body does not discover keys from objects, prefixes or provider output. The following lines refer to the 1241-line frozen composition, not the wrapper's expanded DATA string.

| Canonical key | Load lines | Store lines | New physical key (N is the approved fresh 32-lowercase-hex nonce) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `d01_launcher_release` | 8 | none inside composition; outer wrapper seeds once | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_launcher_release` |
| `d01_launcher_observation_record` | none | 13 | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_launcher_observation_record` |
| `d01_preparation_seed` | 171 twice, 375 | 233 | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_preparation_seed` |
| `d01_preparation_observation_record` | 719 twice (`pObsKey`) | 387 (`pObsKey`) | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_preparation_observation_record` |
| `d01_immediate_owned_handles` | 377, 740 | 703, 867, 1025, 1063, 1116, 1141 | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_immediate_owned_handles` |
| `d01_fresh_password_input` | 718 twice, 1146, 1147, 1148 | 526, 698, 927, 1155 | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_fresh_password_input` |
| `d01_immediate_observation_record` | 759 (`OBSKEY`) | 773 (`OBSKEY`) | `d01_scoped_v1:N:d01_immediate_observation_record` |

A call-site count counts the single nested function's source call once, not how often the function might run. The outer release store is a new invocation-input write, outside the 28 unchanged body calls; it is not an additional canonical key. Unknown names, physical-name strings, non-strings and `__proto__` are rejected before host access. No prefix-matching allowance or enumeration of the host store is added.

## Exact invocation and readback contract

Root first reviews this entire wrapper and immutable frozen body. A future caller provides exactly seven outer fields: `nonce`, `release`, `tools`, `load`, `store`, `text`, `exit`. The nonce is public freshness/ownership metadata, not authentication or a runtime-release token. It must be a root-owned fresh 32-lowercase-hex DATA value, with no actual nonce chosen or generated here. `release` is the original closed 14-field public record: runtime/single-controller/Driver preflight are explicitly true, exact four source/Driver pins match, session is bounded, and all six JSON basenames are bounded/distinct. The outer validator is byte-identical to the original release validator; the original inner check remains in place. No private password/PID/seed/observer input is accepted as a new outer field.

Source is not an invocation argument. The complete fixed body is a literal immutable string in the wrapper archive below. Root must verify the exact wrapper/hash from this immutable report before future execution; the in-wrapper length guard is not a cryptographic verifier or permission grant. The template, helper readcap/size/SHA, original verifier/artifacts, archive-source receipts, source paths, ownership, all bounded command/JSON collectors, output caps and private-input carrier remain their original literal values. They are still checked by the original body, not vouched for by namespace freshness. Its fixed `L_WORKSPACE` remains `/Users/dominik/orca/projects/riAuth-public-preview-sol-management-wave30`; this WT42 design does not silently rebind that runtime workspace or transfer its ownership. Root would reserve the appropriate original fixture owner separately. The 840-second active / 900-second inclusive, 180-second preparation, 600-second helper, 180-second pending, 5-second I/O and essential 60-second cleanup-method limits are unchanged; namespace routing does not renew a lease or establish whole60.

On valid public input, before any store, the adapter synchronously checks **all seven** physical keys for strict absence. It only compares each returned value with `undefined`, does not inspect a colliding record's properties, retain it, print it or read the old canonical key. Any collision including `null` refuses with a fixed tag and no write. Only after all seven checks pass does the wrapper delegate the public release store to its scoped key. The other six stay absent until original composition calls write them. There is no seed/handle/secret reset, null seed, migration, delete, preload from history or alternate accepted input.

One AsyncFunction receives exactly `tools, load, store, text, exit` and the exact original 113395-byte source. Its `load` and `store` are the same scoped references throughout launcher, preparation and the immediate suffix in that body. Arguments/results are delegated directly; there is no clone/stringify/cache/shadow-store layer. This preserves value and transient pass-reference identity at the adapter/host call boundary and the original host's serialization semantics. Root tools/text/exit references also pass unchanged, with no tool fallback. The wrapper has no catch/retry/output/error-string echo and never restarts a clock or transforms an original refusal into a success.

The existing preparation’s validated password store at line 698 goes ONLY to the fresh scoped password key. Original cleanup/after-password clear calls at 526/927/1155 go to that same key; this wrapper adds no password store/read/copy or canonical-global secret copy. Existing typed-input/private-carrier custody, raw-result disposal and cleanup-first-before-await semantics are unchanged. No wrapper output or metadata includes the password, handles, seed, raw controller text or namespace collision value.

Later durable readback uses **the same root-retained nonce and host principal**, through `d01ReadObservation`, and only the three observation keys. It never reopens a fresh-start scope or reads seed/handles/password/release. `undefined` means an observation has not been retained; it is never interpreted as exit0, success, cleanup or absence. The readback rejects wrong key/null/wrong schema, returns the unchanged original source-redacted record and does not print/grade it. Root still applies the original finite outcome/receipt/first-failure/cleanup checks; this small schema tag check is not a sanitizer for an adversarial host store. There is no new public evidence schema, success predicate or allowance. Earlier global observation entries are not fallbacks.

This design grants exactly one future initial whole-composition invocation per separately released fresh nonce. Calling it again with that nonce encounters the occupied release key and refuses before any new write. Continuations that root may separately release must use the same reviewed scoped references/mapping and original suffix/source gates; this report does not authorize or add a continuation/retry launcher. A host `exit()` may end the entire exec cell before a wrapper return, as before; it is passed through unchanged. Readback uses the durable namespace after that termination, not a wrapper-return claim or an exception-derived numeric exit.

## Concurrency, authority and host-function limits

* The host supplies synchronous trusted `load`/`store` with `undefined` for absence. Its documented behavior must remain correct; a Promise-returning, getter/proxy-bearing, lossy, malicious or unreliable host/input is outside this adapter's proof. Source-bound public release DATA comes from root, not arbitrary active objects.
* There is no compare-and-create/CAS/enumeration interface. Seven reads followed by the release write do **not** atomically reserve a namespace against another writer. Root must allocate a unique nonce and serialize ownership of that namespace with no competing writers from preflight through final readback. If this cannot be guaranteed, stop and request the smallest host atomic-claim interface before implementation; do not pretend a null sentinel or guessed freshness proves it.
* Distinct nonces separate store state; they do not authorize parallel fixture runs on shared ports/Driver/labs or create new permission. Original single-controller, owned PID/Driver binding, private output absence and root runtime reservation still apply. No namespace is reused or repaired after a failure.
* The router is not a sandbox/vault/authentication boundary. It cannot prevent an untrusted host from retaining values or an arbitrary caller from deriving a public physical key. Complete frozen source plus trusted exclusive host/capabilities is required. It creates no additional secret copy, but cannot change the original store backend's custody/serialization.
* Readback needs root to retain the public nonce securely enough to identify this invocation. A lost nonce cannot be repaired by searching old globals, scanning namespaces or copying historical records. Host exceptions propagate; no raw exception is printed by this wrapper and no fallback/retry is implemented. Failure after a real resource starts must still be handled by the unchanged original controller/cleanup contract; memory verification cannot prove installed host reliability or real cleanup.

## Complete readable adapter/wrapper template — UNEXECUTED

The single marked initializer is DATA only, replaced below by the exact JSON string encoding of the complete frozen body. All other template bytes are archived literally. The original public validator is copied byte-identically here so invalid input is rejected before any host read/write; it remains separately present unchanged inside the frozen body.

```javascript
// SOURCE DESIGN ONLY. No invocation appears in this archive.
// Root must validate the complete archived wrapper bytes before any later release.
const D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION = __D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION_JSON_LITERAL__;
const D01_CANONICAL_KEYS = Object.freeze([
  "d01_launcher_release",
  "d01_launcher_observation_record",
  "d01_preparation_seed",
  "d01_preparation_observation_record",
  "d01_immediate_owned_handles",
  "d01_fresh_password_input",
  "d01_immediate_observation_record"
]);
const D01_OBSERVATION_KEYS = Object.freeze([
  "d01_launcher_observation_record",
  "d01_preparation_observation_record",
  "d01_immediate_observation_record"
]);
const D01_OBSERVATION_SCHEMAS = Object.freeze([
  "riauth.d01-owned-launcher/v1",
  "riauth.d01-preparation-adapter/v1",
  "riauth.d01-immediate-observation-cleanup/v1"
]);
function d01Refuse(tag) { throw new Error(tag); }
function d01Namespace(nonce) {
  if(typeof nonce!=="string"||!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(nonce))
    d01Refuse("d01_namespace_unconfirmed");
  return "d01_scoped_v1:"+nonce+":";
}
function d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key) {
  const prefix=d01Namespace(nonce);
  if(typeof key!=="string"||!D01_CANONICAL_KEYS.includes(key))
    d01Refuse("d01_key_unconfirmed");
  return prefix+key;
}
function d01OpenFreshScope(nonce,hostLoad,hostStore) {
  d01Namespace(nonce);
  if(typeof hostLoad!=="function"||typeof hostStore!=="function")
    d01Refuse("d01_store_interface_unconfirmed");
  let occupied=false;
  // Strict undefined only: null also occupies a key. No canonical-global read.
  for(const key of D01_CANONICAL_KEYS)
    if(hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key))!==undefined)occupied=true;
  if(occupied)d01Refuse("d01_namespace_occupied");
  // Delegate directly: no value clone, shadow store, reset or extra secret copy.
  const scopedLoad=key=>hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key));
  const scopedStore=(key,value)=>hostStore(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key),value);
  return Object.freeze({load:scopedLoad,store:scopedStore});
}
// This separate read-only entry needs the SAME root-retained public nonce.
// It deliberately does not repeat fresh-start checks on an occupied namespace.
function d01ReadObservation(nonce,key,hostLoad) {
  d01Namespace(nonce);
  if(typeof hostLoad!=="function")d01Refuse("d01_store_interface_unconfirmed");
  const index=typeof key==="string"?D01_OBSERVATION_KEYS.indexOf(key):-1;
  if(index<0)d01Refuse("d01_observation_key_unconfirmed");
  const value=hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key));
  if(value===undefined)return undefined; // Unreached is not a numeric exit/absence proof.
  if(value===null||typeof value!=="object"||Array.isArray(value)||
     value.schema!==D01_OBSERVATION_SCHEMAS[index])
    d01Refuse("d01_observation_record_unconfirmed");
  return value; // Original source-redacted record; no printing or evidence grading.
}
// Byte-identical outer copy of the original public release check. The original
// inner check remains in D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION and runs again without changes.
const lExact=(v,keys)=>v!==null&&typeof v==="object"&&!Array.isArray(v)&&
  Object.keys(v).length===keys.length&&keys.every(k=>Object.hasOwn(v,k));
function lReleaseValid(c) {
  const keys=["runtime_release","single_controller","session","outer_basename",
    "provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename","payload_basename",
    "launcher_cleanup_basename","helper_sha256","controller_template_sha256",
    "entry_source_sha256","driver_contract_receipt_sha256","driver_readonly_preflight_verified"];
  return lExact(c,keys)&&c.runtime_release===true&&c.single_controller===true&&
    c.driver_readonly_preflight_verified===true&&typeof c.session==="string"&&
    /^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}$/.test(c.session)&&
    ["outer_basename","provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename",
     "payload_basename","launcher_cleanup_basename"].every(k=>
       typeof c[k]==="string"&&/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,123}\.json$/.test(c[k]))&&
    new Set(["outer_basename","provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename",
      "payload_basename","launcher_cleanup_basename"].map(k=>c[k])).size===6&&
    c.helper_sha256==="37d32db6fbd8c2c9b676f691d115ecfd1af893724144361971e86f73f573b406"&&
    c.controller_template_sha256==="2dde53fd3f87508e840fedc88af2b85369854eedd050f77708c8e52f43eac691"&&
    c.entry_source_sha256==="505b1c0fec96e248dc67db46379bd801fa06a2678462aa094fb2fc1bc5ea398f"&&
    c.driver_contract_receipt_sha256==="b8145f029796a2063dfe167f026fe299d85afcaedbec5a7abacbeb5bf1a5ea27";
}
async function d01InvokeFresh(input) {
  const keys=["nonce","release","tools","load","store","text","exit"];
  if(!lExact(input,keys)||!lReleaseValid(input.release)||
     input.tools===null||typeof input.tools!=="object"||Array.isArray(input.tools)||
     typeof input.load!=="function"||typeof input.store!=="function"||
     typeof input.text!=="function"||typeof input.exit!=="function"||
     D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION.length!==113395)
    d01Refuse("d01_invocation_input_unconfirmed");
  d01Namespace(input.nonce);
  const scope=d01OpenFreshScope(input.nonce,input.load,input.store);
  // Only this separately approved public release is seeded, after all seven
  // absence checks. Preparation seed/handles/password/observations stay absent.
  scope.store("d01_launcher_release",input.release);
  const AsyncFunction=Object.getPrototypeOf(async function(){}).constructor;
  const invocation=new AsyncFunction("tools","load","store","text","exit",
    D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION);
  // One body, same two scoped references from launcher through immediate suffix.
  // No catch/retry/fallback/clock reset; the original exit and outcome are retained.
  return await invocation(input.tools,scope.load,scope.store,input.text,input.exit);
}
```

    d01Refuse("d01_store_interface_unconfirmed");
  let occupied=false;
  // Strict undefined only: null also occupies a key. No canonical-global read.
  for(const key of D01_CANONICAL_KEYS)
    if(hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key))!==undefined)occupied=true;
  if(occupied)d01Refuse("d01_namespace_occupied");
  // Delegate directly: no value clone, shadow store, reset or extra secret copy.
  const scopedLoad=key=>hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key));
  const scopedStore=(key,value)=>hostStore(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key),value);
  return Object.freeze({load:scopedLoad,store:scopedStore});
}
// This separate read-only entry needs the SAME root-retained public nonce.
// It deliberately does not repeat fresh-start checks on an occupied namespace.
function d01ReadObservation(nonce,key,hostLoad) {
  d01Namespace(nonce);

  const index=typeof key==="string"?D01_OBSERVATION_KEYS.indexOf(key):-1;
  if(index<0)d01Refuse("d01_observation_key_unconfirmed");
  const value=hostLoad(d01PhysicalKey(nonce,key));
  if(value===undefined)return undefined; // Unreached is not a numeric exit/absence proof.
  if(value===null||typeof value!=="object"||Array.isArray(value)||
     value.schema!==D01_OBSERVATION_SCHEMAS[index])

  return value; // Original source-redacted record; no printing or evidence grading.
}
// Byte-identical outer copy of the original public release check. The original
// inner check remains in D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION and runs again without changes.
const lExact=(v,keys)=>v!==null&&typeof v==="object"&&!Array.isArray(v)&&
  Object.keys(v).length===keys.length&&keys.every(k=>Object.hasOwn(v,k));
function lReleaseValid(c) {
  const keys=["runtime_release","single_controller","session","outer_basename",
    "provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename","payload_basename",
    "launcher_cleanup_basename","helper_sha256","controller_template_sha256",
    "entry_source_sha256","driver_contract_receipt_sha256","driver_readonly_preflight_verified"];
  return lExact(c,keys)&&c.runtime_release===true&&c.single_controller===true&&
    c.driver_readonly_preflight_verified===true&&typeof c.session==="string"&&
    /^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}$/.test(c.session)&&
    ["outer_basename","provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename",
     "payload_basename","launcher_cleanup_basename"].every(k=>
       typeof c[k]==="string"&&/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,123}\.json$/.test(c[k]))&&
    new Set(["outer_basename","provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename",
      "payload_basename","launcher_cleanup_basename"].map(k=>c[k])).size===6&&
    c.helper_sha256==="37d32db6fbd8c2c9b676f691d115ecfd1af893724144361971e86f73f573b406"&&
    c.controller_template_sha256==="2dde53fd3f87508e840fedc88af2b85369854eedd050f77708c8e52f43eac691"&&
    c.entry_source_sha256==="505b1c0fec96e248dc67db46379bd801fa06a2678462aa094fb2fc1bc5ea398f"&&
    c.driver_contract_receipt_sha256==="b8145f029796a2063dfe167f026fe299d85afcaedbec5a7abacbeb5bf1a5ea27";

async function d01InvokeFresh(input) {
  const keys=["nonce","release","tools","load","store","text","exit"];
  if(!lExact(input,keys)||!lReleaseValid(input.release)||
     input.tools===null||typeof input.tools!=="object"||Array.isArray(input.tools)||
     typeof input.load!=="function"||typeof input.store!=="function"||
     typeof input.text!=="function"||typeof input.exit!=="function"||
     D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION.length!==113395)
    d01Refuse("d01_invocation_input_unconfirmed");
  d01Namespace(input.nonce);
  const scope=d01OpenFreshScope(input.nonce,input.load,input.store);
  // Only this separately approved public release is seeded, after all seven
  // absence checks. Preparation seed/handles/password/observations stay absent.
  scope.store("d01_launcher_release",input.release);
  const AsyncFunction=Object.getPrototypeOf(async function(){}).constructor;
  const invocation=new AsyncFunction("tools","load","store","text","exit",
    D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION);

  // No catch/retry/fallback/clock reset; the original exit and outcome are retained.
  return await invocation(input.tools,scope.load,scope.store,input.text,input.exit);

    if(n.type==="CallExpression")calls.push(n);});
  check(constructors.length===1&&constructors[0].arguments.length===6&&
    JSON.stringify(constructors[0].arguments.slice(0,5).map(n=>n.value))===
      JSON.stringify(["tools","load","store","text","exit"])&&
    constructors[0].arguments[5].name==="D01_FROZEN_COMPOSITION");
  const seed=calls.find(n=>n.callee.type==="MemberExpression"&&n.callee.object.name==="scope"&&
    n.callee.property.name==="store");
  const finalCall=calls.find(n=>n.callee.name==="invocation");
  const open=calls.find(n=>n.callee.name==="d01OpenFreshScope");
  check(seed.arguments[0].value===KEYS[0]&&open.start<seed.start&&seed.start<constructors[0].start);
  check(finalCall.arguments.length===5&&finalCall.arguments.map(n=>WRAPPER.slice(n.start,n.end)).join(",")===
    "input.tools,scope.load,scope.store,input.text,input.exit");
  check(statements[1].type==="IfStatement"&&statements[1].start<open.start&&
    statements[2].expression.callee.name==="d01Namespace");
  // The guards are proved in the hash-bound source, not by a replaced mock body.
  return Object.freeze({tree,frozen});
}
function host() {
  const data=new Map(),loads=[],stores=[];
  for(const key of KEYS)data.set(key,Object.freeze({private:SENTINEL}));
  return {data,loads,stores,load:key=>{loads.push(key);return data.get(key);},
    store:(key,value)=>{stores.push([key,value]);data.set(key,value);}};
}
const physical=(n,k)=>"d01_scoped_v1:"+n+":"+k;
function globalsUntouched(h,before) {
  check(KEYS.every(k=>h.data.get(k)===before.get(k)));
  check(h.loads.every(k=>!KEYS.includes(k))&&h.stores.every(([k])=>!KEYS.includes(k)));
}
function syncRefusal(fn,tag) {
  let refused=false;try{fn();}catch(e){refused=e!==null&&typeof e==="object"&&e.message===tag;}
  check(refused);
}
async function asyncRefusal(fn,tag) {
  let refused=false;try{await fn();}catch(e){refused=e!==null&&typeof e==="object"&&e.message===tag;}
  check(refused);
}
function release() {
  return {runtime_release:true,single_controller:true,driver_readonly_preflight_verified:true,
    session:"synthetic-memory-only",outer_basename:"a.json",provider_basename:"b.json",
    event_basename:"c.json",cleanup_basename:"d.json",payload_basename:"e.json",
    launcher_cleanup_basename:"f.json",helper_sha256:"37d32db6fbd8c2c9b676f691d115ecfd1af893724144361971e86f73f573b406",
    controller_template_sha256:"2dde53fd3f87508e840fedc88af2b85369854eedd050f77708c8e52f43eac691",
    entry_source_sha256:"505b1c0fec96e248dc67db46379bd801fa06a2678462aa094fb2fc1bc5ea398f",
    driver_contract_receipt_sha256:"b8145f029796a2063dfe167f026fe299d85afcaedbec5a7abacbeb5bf1a5ea27"};
}
function input(h,r=release()) {
  return {nonce:N,release:r,tools:{exec_command:()=>{toolCalls++;throw Error(SENTINEL);}},
    load:h.load,store:h.store,text:()=>{sinkCalls++;throw Error(SENTINEL);},
    exit:()=>{sinkCalls++;throw Error(SENTINEL);}};
}
async function group(name,fn) {
  if(firstFailure!==null)return;
  active={name,passed:false,checks:0};cases.push(active);
  try{await fn();active.passed=true;}catch{firstFailure=name;}
  active=null;
}
function emit(tag=null) {
  if(emitted)return;emitted=true;clearTimeout(timer);
  if(firstFailure===null&&tag!==null)firstFailure=tag;
  const packet={schema:"riauth.d01-scoped-adapter-memory/v1",bindings:EXPECTED,
    status:firstFailure===null?"passed":"failed",first_failure:firstFailure,
    cases,assertions:checks,preflight_checks:preflightChecks,privacy_verified:false,
    constructor_executions:constructorExecutions,frozen_body_executions:frozenExecutions,
    synthetic_tool_calls:toolCalls,public_sink_calls:sinkCalls,elapsed_ms:elapsed()};
  let raw=JSON.stringify(packet);
  const privateFree=!raw.includes(SENTINEL)&&bytes(raw)<=PACKET_CAP;
  if(!privateFree) {
    // No untrusted/sentinel-bearing candidate JSON is printed or clipped.
    const refusal='{"schema":"riauth.d01-scoped-adapter-privacy-refusal/v1","refused":true}\n';
    process.stdout.write(refusal,()=>process.exit(1));return;
  }
  packet.privacy_verified=true;
  raw=JSON.stringify(packet)+"\n";
  if(bytes(raw)>PACKET_CAP){process.exitCode=1;process.exit(1);return;}
  process.stdout.write(raw,()=>process.exit(packet.status==="passed"?0:1));
}
const timer=setTimeout(()=>emit("child_deadline"),29000);
process.on("uncaughtException",()=>emit("harness_internal_error"));
process.on("unhandledRejection",()=>emit("harness_internal_error"));
async function main() {
  const proof=sourceProof();
  const context=vm.createContext(Object.create(null),{codeGeneration:{strings:false,wasm:false}});
  new vm.Script(WRAPPER,{filename:"d01.scoped.immutable.declarations"}).runInContext(context,{timeout:1000});
  api=new vm.Script("Object.freeze({d01OpenFreshScope,d01ReadObservation,d01InvokeFresh,d01Namespace,d01PhysicalKey,lReleaseValid})")
    .runInContext(context,{timeout:1000});
  await group(NAMES[0],()=>{
    const h=host(),old=new Map(h.data),scope=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);
    check(JSON.stringify(h.loads)===JSON.stringify(KEYS.map(k=>physical(N,k)))&&h.stores.length===0);
    const r=release();scope.store(KEYS[0],r);
    check(h.stores.length===1&&h.stores[0][0]===physical(N,KEYS[0])&&h.stores[0][1]===r);
    check(KEYS.slice(1).every(k=>h.data.get(physical(N,k))===undefined));globalsUntouched(h,old);
  });
  await group(NAMES[1],async()=>{
    for(const key of KEYS)for(const value of [null,Object.freeze({private:SENTINEL})]) {
      const h=host(),old=new Map(h.data);h.data.set(physical(N,key),value);
      syncRefusal(()=>api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store),"d01_namespace_occupied");
      check(JSON.stringify(h.loads)===JSON.stringify(KEYS.map(k=>physical(N,k)))&&h.stores.length===0);
      h.loads.length=0;
      // Only this occupied input path may call d01InvokeFresh with valid release DATA.
      await asyncRefusal(()=>api.d01InvokeFresh(input(h)),"d01_namespace_occupied");
      check(JSON.stringify(h.loads)===JSON.stringify(KEYS.map(k=>physical(N,k)))&&h.stores.length===0);
      check(h.data.get(physical(N,key))===value);globalsUntouched(h,old);
    }
  });
  await group(NAMES[2],async()=>{
    const h=host(),scope=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);h.loads.length=0;
    const unknown=[undefined,null,0,false,{},"__proto__","d01_unknown",physical(N,KEYS[0])];
    for(const key of unknown){syncRefusal(()=>scope.load(key),"d01_key_unconfirmed");
      syncRefusal(()=>scope.store(key,SENTINEL),"d01_key_unconfirmed");}
    check(h.loads.length===0&&h.stores.length===0);
    for(const key of [...unknown,KEYS[0],KEYS[2],KEYS[4],KEYS[5]])
      syncRefusal(()=>api.d01ReadObservation(N,key,h.load),"d01_observation_key_unconfirmed");
    check(h.loads.length===0&&h.stores.length===0);
    const badNonces=[undefined,null,0,"",N.slice(1),N+"1","A".repeat(32),"../"+N,N+":"];
    for(const nonce of badNonces){const x=host(),arg=input(x);arg.nonce=nonce;
      await asyncRefusal(()=>api.d01InvokeFresh(arg),"d01_namespace_unconfirmed");
      check(x.loads.length===0&&x.stores.length===0);}
    // All mutations below make the original initial public-input check false.
    const mutations=[r=>{delete r.runtime_release;},r=>{r.extra=true;},
      ...["runtime_release","single_controller","driver_readonly_preflight_verified"].map(k=>r=>{r[k]=false;}),
      ...["helper_sha256","controller_template_sha256","entry_source_sha256","driver_contract_receipt_sha256"].map(k=>r=>{r[k]="0".repeat(64);}),
      r=>{r.session="x".repeat(65);},r=>{r.session="/";},r=>{r.session=0;},
      ...["outer_basename","provider_basename","event_basename","cleanup_basename","payload_basename","launcher_cleanup_basename"]
        .flatMap(k=>[r=>{r[k]="/bad.json";},r=>{r[k]="x".repeat(124)+".json";},r=>{r[k]=0;}]),
      r=>{r.provider_basename=r.outer_basename;}];
    for(const mutate of mutations){const x=host(),r=release();mutate(r);
      check(api.lReleaseValid(r)===false);
      await asyncRefusal(()=>api.d01InvokeFresh(input(x,r)),"d01_invocation_input_unconfirmed");
      check(x.loads.length===0&&x.stores.length===0);}
    for(const field of ["load","store","text","exit","tools"]){const x=host(),arg=input(x);arg[field]=null;
      await asyncRefusal(()=>api.d01InvokeFresh(arg),"d01_invocation_input_unconfirmed");
      check(x.loads.length===0&&x.stores.length===0);}
    const x=host(),arg=input(x);arg.extra=true;
    await asyncRefusal(()=>api.d01InvokeFresh(arg),"d01_invocation_input_unconfirmed");
    check(x.loads.length===0&&x.stores.length===0);
  });
  await group(NAMES[3],()=>{
    const h=host(),a=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store),b=api.d01OpenFreshScope(M,h.load,h.store);
    const loadReference=a.load,storeReference=a.store;
    const value={private:SENTINEL},pass=()=>"synthetic-return",values=[value,pass];
    for(const v of values){a.store(KEYS[2],v);check(a.load(KEYS[2])===v&&h.stores.at(-1)[1]===v);}
    check(b.load(KEYS[2])===undefined);value.changed=true;
    a.store(KEYS[4],value);check(a.load(KEYS[4])===value&&a.load(KEYS[4]).changed===true);
    check(a.load===loadReference&&a.store===storeReference);
  });
  await group(NAMES[4],()=>{
    const h=host(),a=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);a.store(KEYS[0],release());
    const writes=h.stores.length;h.loads.length=0;
    syncRefusal(()=>api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store),"d01_namespace_occupied");
    check(h.stores.length===writes&&h.loads.length===7);
    const b=api.d01OpenFreshScope(M,h.load,h.store);check(b.load(KEYS[0])===undefined);
    // Controlled race exposes the limit: no claim of atomic ownership is made.
    const race=host(),competing={private:SENTINEL};let seen=0;
    const racedLoad=k=>{const v=race.load(k);if(++seen===7)race.data.set(physical(N,KEYS[2]),competing);return v;};
    const r=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,racedLoad,race.store);
    check(r.load(KEYS[2])===competing&&race.stores.length===0);
  });
  await group(NAMES[5],()=>{
    const h=host(),old=new Map(h.data),a=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);
    check(a.load(KEYS[2])===undefined&&a.load(KEYS[4])===undefined&&a.load(KEYS[5])===undefined);
    const seed={private:SENTINEL};a.store(KEYS[2],seed);a.store(KEYS[5],SENTINEL);
    check(a.load(KEYS[5])===SENTINEL);a.store(KEYS[5],null);
    check(a.load(KEYS[5])===null&&a.load(KEYS[2])===seed);
    check(h.data.get(physical(M,KEYS[5]))===undefined);globalsUntouched(h,old);
  });
  await group(NAMES[6],()=>{
    const h=host(),a=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);
    const retained=OBS_DATA.map(v=>({...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v)),first_failure:"synthetic_refusal"}));
    retained.forEach((v,i)=>a.store(OBS[i],v));h.loads.length=0;
    // New VM, same synthetic durable host and nonce; no new fresh-open operation.
    const second=vm.createContext(Object.create(null),{codeGeneration:{strings:false,wasm:false}});
    new vm.Script(WRAPPER).runInContext(second,{timeout:1000});
    const read=new vm.Script("d01ReadObservation").runInContext(second,{timeout:1000});
    for(let i=0;i<OBS.length;i++){const v=read(N,OBS[i],h.load);check(v===retained[i]&&
      v.first_failure==="synthetic_refusal"&&v.first_event_proven===false&&
      v.whole_cleanup_within60_proven===false&&v.controller_exit===null&&v.final_absence===null&&
      (!Object.hasOwn(v,"journey_credit")||v.journey_credit===false)&&
      (!Object.hasOwn(v,"resource_release_proven")||v.resource_release_proven===false));}
    check(JSON.stringify(h.loads)===JSON.stringify(OBS.map(k=>physical(N,k))));
    check(read(M,OBS[0],h.load)===undefined);
    for(const v of [null,{schema:"wrong"}]){h.data.set(physical(N,OBS[0]),v);
      syncRefusal(()=>read(N,OBS[0],h.load),"d01_observation_record_unconfirmed");}
  });
  await group(NAMES[7],()=>{
    check(bytes(proof.frozen)===EXPECTED.frozen_bytes&&digest(proof.frozen)===EXPECTED.frozen_sha256);
    check(constructorExecutions===0&&frozenExecutions===0&&toolCalls===0&&sinkCalls===0);
    check(api.lReleaseValid(release())===true);
  });
  await group(NAMES[8],()=>{
    const h=host(),scope=api.d01OpenFreshScope(N,h.load,h.store);let caught=false;
    const badLoad=()=>{throw Error(SENTINEL);};
    try{api.d01ReadObservation(N,OBS[0],badLoad);}catch{caught=true;}
    check(caught); // Raw host error is deliberately never copied to a packet/sink.
    check(h.stores.length===0&&toolCalls===0&&sinkCalls===0);
    check(!JSON.stringify({names:cases.map(c=>c.name),checks}).includes(SENTINEL));
  });
  if(firstFailure===null)check(cases.length===NAMES.length&&cases.every((c,i)=>c.name===NAMES[i]&&c.passed&&c.checks>0));
  emit();
}
main().catch(()=>emit("harness_internal_error"));

Readable logic/template bytes `20272`, SHA256 `c95b0489cf352720d80ce06b1e8609fc8ca7dba19a18bd43733ef4c4e98d6653`. The complete child archive is losslessly embedded in the supervisor PAYLOAD_B64 DATA initializer below. Decoding that initializer, requiring exact length/hash, then reversing the TWO string initializers must reconstruct this entire readable template AND the entire original wrapper/template bytes. No archived assembly function is called.

### Complete sole-child supervisor — UNEXECUTED

Bytes `226923`, SHA256 `2a8be4dcad567d8e16a6a3ed1dc1bd0ef845d565e4db0c70be8834abcfad85e9`. One Popen site, `start_new_session=True`; SIGCHLD is explicitly default before it. No poll/wait/communicate or external helper exists. Ownership uses exact live pgid OR exact terminal WNOWAIT event with unreaped leader before SIGKILL; EPERM merely defers that observation, never supplies a zero exit. When live/terminal identity is unavailable, it sends no destructive signal; it still attempts consuming waitpid of the exact sole Popen child. Only returned exact pid + exited/signaled wait status establishes numeric code/reap. Group absence is an independent post-reap signal-zero existence query, not a delivered signal; existing/reused/unqueryable group refuses and is never killed after reap. No SIGKILL follows consuming wait. Normal EOF gets a bounded natural-exit wait first, so pipe closure alone does not race a destructive signal. A waitpid/invalid-event/non-EPERM waitid error disables destructive signaling; ECHILD is not ignored in favor of a potentially reused pgid. Final public output uses one capped nonblocking write and the numeric return still checks the outer clock; public `accepted:true` alone is never acceptance if actual exit is nonzero. If bounded join/absence cannot be proved, capture/result stay failed.

Raw stdout/stderr and complete bounded packet bytes are exclusive0600, fsynced/closed first; a fixed capture receipt retains true numeric code, EOF, capped-input/output, reap/group, source hashes and only finite errno/identity fields before packet parsing/grading. Null/absence is never exit0. Parent JSON duplicate/type/nonbool/bounds/schema/ordered-group validation is closed; no child error/trace/value is echoed. Child-public packet is finite names/counts/flags/source identities; synthetic secret/error sentinels never enter it. If the child privacy check fails, only a separate fixed privacy-refusal packet is emitted and exits1; it does not print the rejected packet or clip counters. Raw failures are kept privately capped without public echo. The private capture includes bounded actual child/group PID, exact waitpid-match flag/raw wait status and signal attempt/delivery booleans; no PID or raw child error enters public output. Signal-after-reap0 is a source-order invariant, not a separate kernel tracer. Partial/overflow/incomplete output refuses. EOF flags are set both in the normal read and final drain. No weakened comparison or forced result.

NAMES=['fresh_globals_untouched', 'occupied_all_seven_no_write', 'closed_keys_and_input_bounds', 'direct_reference_and_state', 'nonce_collision_no_reuse', 'private_state_separation', 'durable_readback_false_unreached', 'immutable_source_and_boundary', 'finite_privacy_failure']
CHILD_DEADLINE=START+30_000_000_000
CLEANUP_DEADLINE=START+32_000_000_000
OUTER_DEADLINE=START+35_000_000_000
FLOOR=8*1024**3
START_FREE=17*1024**3//2
OUT_CAP=32768
ERR_CAP=16384
CHECK_CAP=512
first_failure=None
ALLOWED_FAILURES={"preflight_failed","spawn_failed","capture_failed","child_deadline","disk_floor",
    "child_input_closed","child_output_cap","owned_identity_unconfirmed","owned_signal_failed",
    "owned_reap_unconfirmed","owned_group_not_empty","capture_incomplete","evidence_failed",
    "packet_refused","child_failed","outer_deadline"}
def latch(tag):
    global first_failure
    if tag not in ALLOWED_FAILURES:tag="capture_failed"
    if first_failure is None:first_failure=tag

def true_int(v,lo,hi):return type(v) is int and lo<=v<=hi

def deadline():
    if time.monotonic_ns()>=OUTER_DEADLINE:raise TimeoutError()

def encode(v):return (json.dumps(v,sort_keys=True,separators=(",",":"),allow_nan=False)+"\n").encode("ascii")

def save(directory,name,data):
    deadline()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z_]+\.(?:json|raw|py|js)",name) or len(data)>524288:raise ValueError()
    fd=os.open(directory/name,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd,"wb",closefd=False) as out:
            out.write(data);out.flush();os.fsync(out.fileno())
        info=os.fstat(fd)
        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid!=os.getuid() or info.st_nlink!=1 or stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode)!=0o600:raise ValueError()
    finally:os.close(fd)
    deadline()
    return {"bytes":len(data),"sha256":hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),"mode":384}

def terminal(info,pid):
    return (info is not None and true_int(info.si_pid,1,2147483647) and info.si_pid==pid and
        type(info.si_signo) is int and info.si_signo==signal.SIGCHLD and
        type(info.si_code) is int and info.si_code in (os.CLD_EXITED,os.CLD_KILLED,os.CLD_DUMPED) and
        true_int(info.si_status,0,255) and (info.si_code==os.CLD_EXITED or info.si_status>0))

def cleanup(child):
    # Sole Popen child, default SIGCHLD, no implicit/other consuming reaper.
    code=None;reaped=False;empty=False;owned=False;can_signal=True
    diag={"identity":"unconfirmed","waitid_errno":None,"waitpid_errno":None,
        "signal_errno":None,"absence_errno":None,"signals_after_reap":0,
        "signal_attempted":False,"signal_delivered":False,"waitpid_exact":False,"wait_status":None}
    pid=child.pid
    eligible=true_int(pid,1,2147483647) and child.returncode is None
    if not eligible or signal.getsignal(signal.SIGCHLD)!=signal.SIG_DFL:
        latch("owned_identity_unconfirmed");return code,reaped,empty,diag
    def consume_ready():
        nonlocal code,reaped,can_signal
        try:
            waited,status=os.waitpid(pid,os.WNOHANG)
            if waited==0:return
            if waited!=pid or not (os.WIFEXITED(status) or os.WIFSIGNALED(status)):raise ValueError()
            code=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
            if not true_int(code,-255,255):raise ValueError()
            child.returncode=code;reaped=True
            diag["waitpid_exact"]=True;diag["wait_status"]=status
            if diag["identity"]=="unconfirmed":diag["identity"]="consuming_waitpid"
        except OSError as e:
            diag["waitpid_errno"]=e.errno if true_int(e.errno,1,4095) else None
            can_signal=False;latch("owned_reap_unconfirmed")
        except Exception:can_signal=False;latch("owned_reap_unconfirmed")
    # EOF is not process exit: allow normal exit without racing a SIGKILL.
    if first_failure is None:
        while time.monotonic_ns()<CHILD_DEADLINE and not reaped and can_signal:
            consume_ready()
            if not reaped and can_signal:
                time.sleep(min(.005,max(0,(CHILD_DEADLINE-time.monotonic_ns())/1e9)))
        if not reaped and can_signal:latch("child_deadline")
    if not reaped and can_signal:
        try:
            info=os.waitid(os.P_PID,pid,os.WEXITED|os.WNOHANG|os.WNOWAIT)
            if info is not None:
                if not terminal(info,pid):raise ValueError()
                owned=True;diag["identity"]="wnowait"
        except OSError as e:
            diag["waitid_errno"]=e.errno if true_int(e.errno,1,4095) else None
            # EPERM defers observation; ECHILD/other errors never allow signaling.
            if e.errno!=errno.EPERM:can_signal=False;latch("owned_identity_unconfirmed")
        except Exception:can_signal=False;latch("owned_identity_unconfirmed")
        if not owned and can_signal:
            try:
                if os.getpgid(pid)==pid:owned=True;diag["identity"]="live_group"
            except ProcessLookupError:pass
            except Exception:can_signal=False;latch("owned_identity_unconfirmed")
        if owned and can_signal and child.returncode is None:
            # WNOWAIT or exact live group holds the unreaped leader against reuse.
            diag["signal_attempted"]=True
            try:os.killpg(pid,signal.SIGKILL);diag["signal_delivered"]=True
            except ProcessLookupError:pass
            except OSError as e:
                diag["signal_errno"]=e.errno if true_int(e.errno,1,4095) else None;latch("owned_signal_failed")
            except Exception:latch("owned_signal_failed")
        # Lack of identity sends NO signal; consuming the exact child remains safe.
    while time.monotonic_ns()<CLEANUP_DEADLINE and not reaped:
        consume_ready()
        if not reaped:
            time.sleep(min(.005,max(0,(CLEANUP_DEADLINE-time.monotonic_ns())/1e9)))
    if not reaped:latch("owned_reap_unconfirmed")
    if reaped:
        # Signal zero is only a non-delivering existence query, never destruction.
        # A group existing/reusing this number refuses and is never signaled again.
        try:os.killpg(pid,0)
        except ProcessLookupError:empty=True
        except OSError as e:diag["absence_errno"]=e.errno if true_int(e.errno,1,4095) else None
        if not empty:latch("owned_group_not_empty")
    return code,reaped,empty,diag

def duplicate_free(pairs):
    d={}
    for key,value in pairs:
        if key in d:raise ValueError()
        d[key]=value
    return d

def grade_packet(raw):
    if not 0<len(raw)<=OUT_CAP or not raw.endswith(b"\n"):raise ValueError()
    d=json.loads(raw.decode("ascii"),object_pairs_hook=duplicate_free,
        parse_constant=lambda _:(_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError()))
    fields={"schema","bindings","status","first_failure","cases","assertions","preflight_checks",
        "privacy_verified","constructor_executions","frozen_body_executions","synthetic_tool_calls",
        "public_sink_calls","elapsed_ms"}
    if type(d) is not dict or set(d)!=fields or d["schema"]!="riauth.d01-scoped-adapter-memory/v1":raise ValueError()
    if type(d["bindings"]) is not dict or set(d["bindings"])!=set(BINDINGS):raise ValueError()
    for key,value in BINDINGS.items():
        if type(d["bindings"][key]) is not type(value) or d["bindings"][key]!=value:raise ValueError()
    if d["status"] not in ("passed","failed") or not (d["first_failure"] is None or
        type(d["first_failure"]) is str and d["first_failure"] in NAMES+["child_deadline","harness_internal_error","privacy_packet_refusal"]):raise ValueError()
    if not true_int(d["assertions"],1,CHECK_CAP) or not true_int(d["preflight_checks"],1,CHECK_CAP):raise ValueError()
    if not true_int(d["elapsed_ms"],0,30000) or type(d["privacy_verified"]) is not bool:raise ValueError()
    for key in ("constructor_executions","frozen_body_executions","synthetic_tool_calls","public_sink_calls"):
        if not true_int(d[key],0,0):raise ValueError()
    cases=d["cases"]
    if type(cases) is not list or not 0<=len(cases)<=len(NAMES):raise ValueError()
    for i,c in enumerate(cases):
        if type(c) is not dict or set(c)!={"name","passed","checks"} or c["name"]!=NAMES[i] or type(c["passed"]) is not bool or not true_int(c["checks"],1,CHECK_CAP):raise ValueError()
    if d["preflight_checks"]+sum(c["checks"] for c in cases)!=d["assertions"]:raise ValueError()
    if not (d["status"]=="passed" and d["first_failure"] is None and len(cases)==len(NAMES) and
        all(c["passed"] for c in cases) and d["privacy_verified"] is True):return False
    return True

def main():
    child=None;sel=None;evidence=None;phase="preflight";spawn_count=0
    raw={"out":bytearray(),"err":bytearray()};eof={"out":False,"err":False};capped=False;sent=0
    code=None;reaped=False;empty=False;diag=None;records={};minimum_free=None;last_disk=0
    candidate=False;retained=False;script_hash=None
    try:
        if len(sys.argv)!=2 or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{16}",sys.argv[1]) is None:raise ValueError()
        if pathlib.Path.cwd()!=ROOT or ROOT.is_symlink():raise ValueError()
        if not all(hasattr(os,k) for k in ("waitid","P_PID","WEXITED","WNOHANG","WNOWAIT")):raise ValueError()
        original=sys.orig_argv
        if len(original)!=4 or original[1]!="-c":raise ValueError()
        script=original[2].encode("ascii")
        if not 0<len(script)<=524288:raise ValueError()
        script_hash=hashlib.sha256(script).hexdigest()
        data=base64.b64decode(PAYLOAD_B64,validate=True)
        if len(data)!=PAYLOAD_BYTES or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=PAYLOAD_SHA:raise ValueError()
        nodefd=os.open(NODE,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW)
        try:
            info=os.fstat(nodefd)
            if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_size!=NODE_BYTES or not info.st_mode&0o111:raise ValueError()
            with os.fdopen(nodefd,"rb",closefd=False) as source:node=source.read(NODE_BYTES+1)
            if len(node)!=NODE_BYTES or hashlib.sha256(node).hexdigest()!=NODE_SHA:raise ValueError()
        finally:os.close(nodefd)
        free=shutil.disk_usage(ROOT).free;minimum_free=free
        if free<START_FREE:raise ValueError()
        private=ROOT/"deployment-private";info=private.lstat()
        if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) or stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode)!=0o700 or info.st_uid!=os.getuid():raise ValueError()
        evidence=private/("d01_scoped_memory_"+sys.argv[1]);os.mkdir(evidence,0o700)
        info=evidence.lstat()
        if not stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) or stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode)!=0o700 or info.st_uid!=os.getuid():raise ValueError()
        records["controller"]=save(evidence,"controller.py",script)
        records["payload"]=save(evidence,"harness.js",data)
        if time.monotonic_ns()>=CHILD_DEADLINE:raise TimeoutError()
        signal.signal(signal.SIGCHLD,signal.SIG_DFL)
        if signal.getsignal(signal.SIGCHLD)!=signal.SIG_DFL:raise ValueError()
        phase="spawn"
        child=subprocess.Popen([str(NODE),"--expose-internals","--input-type=commonjs","-"],
            cwd=evidence,env={"PATH":"/usr/bin:/bin","LANG":"C","LC_ALL":"C"},
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            start_new_session=True,bufsize=0)
        spawn_count=1;phase="capture";sel=selectors.DefaultSelector()
        for stream,kind,event in ((child.stdin,"in",selectors.EVENT_WRITE),
            (child.stdout,"out",selectors.EVENT_READ),(child.stderr,"err",selectors.EVENT_READ)):
            os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(),False);sel.register(stream,event,kind)
        while sel.get_map():
            now=time.monotonic_ns()
            if now>=CHILD_DEADLINE:latch("child_deadline");break
            if now-last_disk>=2_000_000_000:
                free=shutil.disk_usage(ROOT).free;minimum_free=min(minimum_free,free);last_disk=now
                if free<FLOOR:latch("disk_floor");break
            for key,_ in sel.select(min(.02,max(0,(CHILD_DEADLINE-now)/1e9))):
                stream,kind=key.fileobj,key.data
                if kind=="in":
                    try:written=os.write(stream.fileno(),data[sent:sent+16384])
                    except BlockingIOError:continue
                    except BrokenPipeError:latch("child_input_closed");sel.unregister(stream);stream.close();continue
                    sent+=written
                    if sent==len(data):sel.unregister(stream);stream.close()
                else:
                    try:part=os.read(stream.fileno(),4096)
                    except BlockingIOError:continue
                    if not part:eof[kind]=True;sel.unregister(stream);stream.close();continue
                    cap=OUT_CAP if kind=="out" else ERR_CAP
                    if len(raw[kind])+len(part)>cap:
                        raw[kind].extend(part[:cap-len(raw[kind])]);capped=True;latch("child_output_cap");break
                    raw[kind].extend(part)
            if first_failure is not None:break
    except Exception:latch(phase+"_failed")
    finally:
        if child is not None:
            code,reaped,empty,diag=cleanup(child)
            # Only finite nonblocking drain on the same streams, after owned cleanup.
            if sel is not None:
                for key in list(sel.get_map().values()):
                    stream,kind=key.fileobj,key.data
                    if kind in ("out","err"):
                        for _ in range(13):
                            try:part=os.read(stream.fileno(),4096)
                            except BlockingIOError:break
                            except OSError:break
                            if not part:eof[kind]=True;break
                            cap=OUT_CAP if kind=="out" else ERR_CAP
                            if len(raw[kind])+len(part)>cap:
                                raw[kind].extend(part[:cap-len(raw[kind])]);capped=True;latch("child_output_cap");break
                            raw[kind].extend(part)
                    try:sel.unregister(stream);stream.close()
                    except Exception:pass
                sel.close()
        if not (reaped and empty and all(eof.values()) and not capped and sent==PAYLOAD_BYTES):latch("capture_incomplete")
        if evidence is not None:
            try:
                # Full bounded raw bytes + exit/EOF ownership receipts BEFORE any grading.
                records["stdout"]=save(evidence,"stdout.raw",bytes(raw["out"]))
                records["stderr"]=save(evidence,"stderr.raw",bytes(raw["err"]))
                records["packet"]=save(evidence,"packet.json",bytes(raw["out"]))
                capture={"schema":"riauth.d01-scoped-memory-capture/v1","controller_sha256":script_hash,
                    "payload_sha256":PAYLOAD_SHA,"payload_bytes":PAYLOAD_BYTES,"node_sha256":NODE_SHA,
                    "spawn_count":spawn_count,"child_exit":code,"child_reaped":reaped,"group_absent":empty,
                    "owned_child_pid":child.pid if child is not None else None,
                    "owned_group_id":child.pid if child is not None else None,
                    "stream_eof":eof,"output_capped":capped,"input_bytes":sent,
                    "minimum_free_bytes":minimum_free,"cleanup":diag,"first_failure":first_failure,"files":records.copy()}
                records["capture"]=save(evidence,"capture.json",encode(capture))
                retained=True
            except Exception:latch("evidence_failed")
    # No parsing/comparison of the child packet occurs before retention above.
    if retained and first_failure is None:
        try:
            candidate=grade_packet(bytes(raw["out"]))
            if bytes(raw["err"]) or code!=0 or not candidate:latch("child_failed");candidate=False
        except Exception:latch("packet_refused");candidate=False
    review={"schema":"riauth.d01-scoped-memory-review/v1","pre_final_clock_candidate":candidate,
        "acceptance_requires_final_clock":True,"first_failure":first_failure,"child_exit":code,
        "child_reaped":reaped,"group_absent":empty,"capture_retained":retained,"files":records.copy()}
    if evidence is not None:
        try:records["review"]=save(evidence,"review.json",encode(review))
        except Exception:latch("evidence_failed");candidate=False
    # Final clock is AFTER all fsync/close operations; no evidence write follows it.
    finished=time.monotonic_ns();elapsed_ms=(finished-START)//1_000_000
    if finished>=OUTER_DEADLINE:latch("outer_deadline");candidate=False
    accepted=(candidate and first_failure is None and retained and reaped and empty and code==0)
    public={"schema":"riauth.d01-scoped-memory-supervisor/v1","accepted":accepted,
        "first_failure":first_failure,"child_exit":code,"child_reaped":reaped,"group_absent":empty,
        "spawn_count":spawn_count,"capture_retained":retained,"elapsed_ms":elapsed_ms,
        "minimum_free_bytes":minimum_free,"payload_sha256":PAYLOAD_SHA,"controller_sha256":script_hash,
        "capture_sha256":records.get("capture",{}).get("sha256"),"review_sha256":records.get("review",{}).get("sha256")}
    # One capped nonblocking pipe write; no unbounded output flush/fallback.
    public_bytes=encode(public)
    if len(public_bytes)>4096:return 1
    try:
        os.set_blocking(sys.stdout.fileno(),False)
        if os.write(sys.stdout.fileno(),public_bytes)!=len(public_bytes):return 1
    except OSError:return 1
    # Numeric exit is still mandatory; output alone is never acceptance.
    return 0 if accepted and time.monotonic_ns()<OUTER_DEADLINE else 1
if __name__=="__main__":sys.exit(main())
```
